different-ai/openwork ↗ 1 release · 1 stable
v0.18.64OpenWork v0.18.64 Stable2026-11-03T14:33:06Z

What's new

OpenWork v0.18.64 desktop release.

  • Public artifacts use the openwork-* naming convention.
  • Cloud artifacts use openwork-cloud-* and require sign-in without enterprise activation.
  • Enterprise artifacts use openwork-enterprise-* while retaining the standard OpenWork app identity.
  • Public, Cloud, and enterprise Electron builds use separate updater manifests on this release.

Windows installers are signed using Microsoft Artifact Signing.

Known open fixes

Decision: OpenWork release operator, acting on the request to release through GitHub Actions without further questions. Ship the reviewed dev branch as-is; do not merge unrelated open PRs as part of a release. These changes remain deferred for separate review.

HyperFrames v0.9.8

Released on 2026-11-03.

Renders that capture faster with BeginFrame now fall back to screenshot capture when that path fails. A render request can also ask for screenshot capture directly.

Features

  • Studio Server: The preview records the kind and origin of each early error (b84835c41, #7041).
  • Producer: A render request can force screenshot capture for that render (79b6af9b4, #7066).

Fixes

  • Producer: A BeginFrame capture failure re-renders on a screenshot session (9a2cd9e61, #7070).
  • Core: Audio no longer plays fast for a moment after Play (3eead5432, #7067).
  • CLI: Reverse on Free runs billing subscribe credited to the run (3baf81f9a, #7046).

v0.9.7 Stable2026-11-03T05:46:42Z

HyperFrames v0.9.7

Released on 2026-11-03.

Studio playback no longer freezes WebGL scenes when motion blur is on. Preview playback now lands on the same frame as a scrub or an export when a set cuts into a running tween. Your CLI login now takes priority over an API key left in your shell.

Features

  • Lint: overlapping_gsap_tweens now also flags a set that fires while a tween on the same element and property is still running, a pattern that plays differently from how it scrubs and exports (5e30bbdbe, #7059).
  • Preview: A video the browser cannot play, such as HEVC without a decoder or ProRes, shows its own frame at the playhead instead of black while its playable copy is made. Copy progress is now reported at Full quality too (eb3562966, #6589).

Fixes

  • Studio: With motion blur on, a scene that draws to a WebGL canvas no longer freezes during preview playback. Motion blur now falls back for those scenes, as it already did in renders (112ba5330, #7060).
  • Core: Preview playback now lands on the same frame as a scrub or an export when a set fires inside a running tween on the same property. Before, a word hidden just before its pop-in ended could stay on screen while playing (0f85af7c7, #7061).
  • CLI: hyperframes cloud render no longer offers --page-motion-blur, because the hosted render API rejected requests that used it. Turn motion blur on in the composition with data-motion-blur="on". Local hyperframes render --page-motion-blur is unchanged (dffc640e8, #7062).
  • CLI: A session from hyperframes auth login now takes priority over HEYGEN_API_KEY and HYPERFRAMES_API_KEY, so a key left in your shell no longer replaces the account you signed in with. The key still wins when HEYGEN_API_URL or HEYGEN_API_BASE points at another host, or when the login cannot refresh (d03bdeae9, #7051).
  • Studio Server: Two exports of the same project started within one second now get their own jobs and output files. Before, the second export replaced the first (92943d99f, #7053).
  • CLI: hyperframes snapshot no longer crashes Chrome on a project with many clips cut from one 4K video. Like render, it now stops Chrome from decoding local videos it already replaces with extracted frames (81b93cf05, #6949).
  • CLI: The latestVersion value in --json output is never older than the CLI you are running, even right after an upgrade (8a6636efe, #7049).
  • CLI: hyperframes auth login --device now works without a terminal, such as in a coding agent's shell or an SSH command. It still refuses to run in CI (de2603e63, #7045).

Docs & Examples

  • Skills: The reverse skill asks once for a credit top-up that covers the whole run when several steps run out at the same time, and builds its project with init --video (05ddddbbf, #7048).
  • Changelog: Added the weekly update for October 26 to November 2, 2026 (6feb4bec7, #7032).
  • Changelog: The v0.9.6 release notes now list four changes they had missed (4ef8c1de5, #7040).

Internal

  • Studio Server: Raised the package size budget to fit the new preview features (b22af8f25, #7044).

Full changelog

https://github.com/heygen-com/hyperframes/compare/v0.9.6...v0.9.7

First-time preview setup

Existing stable versions do not support --channel. Download the archive for your OS and architecture from this release, verify it against SHA256SUMS, and extract the entire archive into its own directory (keep all Linux companion files together). Run the extracted binary explicitly, using its full path, rather than the old stable jcode on PATH. For Windows, use the extracted .exe.

From that preview binary, run jcode update --channel preview (substitute its full path for jcode) to persist the opt-in channel and receive future previews. To roll back, run the preview binary with update --channel stable, or reinstall the current stable release. Stable users are never moved to previews automatically.

This preview series ends when the v1.0.0 stable tag is created.

code-yeongyu/oh-my-openagent ↗ 1 release · 1 stable
v5.1.59 Stable2026-11-03T09:08:03Z

Runs on senpi 2026.11.2-3: the first message of a new session no longer stalls for several seconds, and agent kill/pkill/killall commands that would hit protected processes are refused. (#12200)

Fixed

  • Memory sync refuses a received item on Windows whose working path git cannot stage (at or past MAX_PATH, which git for Windows opens without core.longpaths), dropping it with a receipt before anything is rendered instead of failing the sync commit and wedging the device on every resume (#12199). POSIX behaviour is unchanged. (#12201)
  • The task widget no longer keeps finished team members as process completed rows. A member that completes its turn leaves the widget even while its handle stays resident; /tasks still lists it and task_send still revives it. (#12160)
  • Memory sync validates an item key before it reaches the working tree, the item store or git, so a peer can no longer land a path outside the memory root or inside the repository's own metadata. A key must be a normalized relative path: .. and . segments, absolute paths, drive and UNC prefixes, backslashes, control and format code points, non-NFC text, over-long keys and segments, Windows device names, trailing dots or spaces, and any component git or a file system would fold to .git are refused. A refused item is dropped on its own with a receipt, a log line that carries the key's byte length and the rule rather than the key, and a rejectedItems count, while the rest of its batch applies and the cursor advances, so one bad item never pauses a device. Local capture uses the same grammar, never follows a symlink out of the memory root, and a file whose name the grammar refuses is reported instead of sent. A path that is valid but spelled with a different Unicode normalization no longer forks one item into two. (#12152, fixes #12147)
  • After a session starts or resumes, workflow (DAG) views now always receive the current run list before the first turn begins, and a client that reconnects to a running session gets it again, so the desktop workflow view no longer stays empty or stale until something changes. A workflow that was still running when the session closed resumes in the background: its state is shown as soon as it is recovered, and the session's first turn no longer waits for the whole workflow to finish. (#12122, fixes #12113)
  • Memory git commands no longer start git's builtin fsmonitor daemon when your git config turns core.fsmonitor on. Every git command memory runs now carries -c core.fsmonitor=false, the clean-tree status, add and diff included, so no background daemon outlives git and keeps the memory repository busy. (#12097, part of #10391)
  • fact verify no longer skips text inside SVG <foreignObject>: the ulw-research tokenizer now parses foreignObject subtrees as HTML (the rest of the SVG stays raw), so fact unit/value mutations there are caught and bare numerals must trace to a chart CSV plot value. (#12040, fixes #11570)
  • task_output no longer replays an unchanged status snapshot to the same caller: a repeated peek at a task whose status has not moved returns no_progress instead of the full record again, which keeps polling loops from refilling the context with identical output. Thanks to @codeg-dev. (#7662)

Changed

  • Every built-in reasoning default that sat at medium or xhigh now sits at high: the default lane daily-normal runs claude-opus-5-5 at high (was medium), daily-heavy and the Fable 5.5 launch rungs drop xhigh for high, and geeky-normal, the deep-low/unspecified/quick category chains, the OpenCode category catalogs, the builtin agent fallback chains and the model-fallback default move with them. Model ids, provider orders and the low/max/off levels are untouched. (#12094, fixes #12085)

Added

  • Add omo trust ensure --json [--wait] [--lease-pid <pid>], answered by the launcher trust stage: it imports within the launch budget, writes the private generation and leases it, so the retention sweep never removes a generation a running process still uses. (#11969, part of #10762)

  • Add the eval-integrity shared skill: a read-only audit of whether an eval, benchmark, metric, A/B test, holdout, LLM judge, or user study can support its stated claim, reported under one PASS / FAIL / INCONCLUSIVE verdict line. Thanks to @realsigridjin. (#7593)

gastownhall/beads ↗ 1 release · 1 prerelease
v1.3.3-rc.1 Prerelease2026-11-03T08:40:45Z

beads v1.3.3-rc.1

Pre-compiled binaries for Linux, macOS (Intel & Apple Silicon), Windows (AMD64 & ARM64), Android/Termux (ARM64), and FreeBSD.

Installation (release candidate)

This is a prerelease for validation. The stable install channels — Homebrew, the install.sh / install.ps1 scripts, npm, and PyPI — intentionally keep serving the latest stable release, so they will not install this RC. Use one of the methods below.

Manual download (recommended): Download the archive for your platform from the Assets below, extract it, and place bd on your PATH.

Go install (pinned to this RC):

go install github.com/steveyegge/beads/cmd/bd@v1.3.3-rc.1
# For embedded Dolt support:
CGO_ENABLED=1 GOFLAGS=-tags=gms_pure_go go install github.com/steveyegge/beads/cmd/bd@v1.3.3-rc.1

When you are done validating, return to the stable release through your normal channel (e.g. brew upgrade beads).

unixwin/niubash ↗ 1 release · 1 stable
v1.5.0Niubash v1.5.0 Stable2026-11-03T08:21:09Z

⚠️ Release assets frozen (2026-11-03). The files attached to this release are final for v1.5.0 and will not be replaced — downstream manifests (winget and others) pin these exact bytes. Please verify downloads against SHA256SUMS. 本发行资产已于 2026-11-03 定版冻结，不再替换（winget 等下游清单按字节哈希引用这些文件）；下载请按 SHA256SUMS 校验。

Niubash v1.5.0 packages for Windows, Linux, and macOS.

Engine: rubash git 29bbf52481c3 — every platform build in this release compiled that same revision, pinned by scripts/release/rubash-pin.json and fail-hard verified at fetch time (rubash#827). The release compiles the pinned engine, not "whatever rubash master was when the pipeline ran". Verify locally with niu --version, which prints the same revision.

Windows (x64, ARM64): Inno Setup installers (*-setup.exe) add user PATH and Windows Terminal profile integration; portable archives bundle niu with WinuxCmd and bash/sh shims.

Portable download guide (niubash#470): prefer *.tar.gz — it is hardlink-native. The bundled payload ships real NTFS hardlinks (the whole git.exe family is one shared binary, awk/gawk likewise, exactly what wpm install lays down), so the tarball is roughly half the zip's download size and unpacks to a much smaller on-disk tree; tar -xf <file>.tar.gz works out of the box on Windows 10 1803+ and any modern Linux/macOS, and on a filesystem without hardlink support (FAT32/exFAT) extraction degrades gracefully to independent byte-identical copies with no loss of function. *.7z is the smallest download (solid LZMA already collapses the shared payloads) but current 7-Zip releases restore the shared families as independent copies — every file is byte-identical, only disk usage grows. The classic *.zip is unchanged, stores every payload in full, and remains for tooling that only speaks zip.

Windows packages come with the must-install bundle pre-installed (niubash#189, niubash#209): niugit (native Windows git — the full plugin distro's five entries install through git, so a fresh machine without git.exe no longer fails niu setup), gawk 5.4.1 with plain awk working too, plus ripgrep (rg) and fd — all landing in the bundled WinuxCmd root as wpm JSON-package payloads, assembled at CI time with no runtime downloads, no runtime archive formats, and no symlinks. Pre-install is best-effort, not a promise: on legs where the package index has no matching artifact (currently niugit and gawk on the ARM64 builds; ripgrep and fd have ARM64 artifacts) the package ships WITHOUT that entry — run winuxcmd wpm install <name> once after installing to add it. The pre-install list and its recorded exclusions live in scripts/release/preinstall.json.

niubash-skill-v<version>.zip is the AI agent skill bundle (SKILL.md + references, WinuxCmd-skill layout): unpack it into an agent skills directory, or let the shell do it — niu skill install embeds the same content.

Linux (x86_64, aarch64; glibc): portable tarballs with the niu binary, README, and LICENSE — Unix builds bundle nothing, native system tools are used. Untar anywhere and run ./niu.

macOS (aarch64, x86_64): portable tarballs with the niu binary, README, and LICENSE. Untar anywhere and run ./niu.

Every artifact was smoke-verified on the build OS of the job that produced it, before upload: niu -c 'echo ok', printf 'a\nb\n' | niu -c 'sort | head -1', an offline NIU_PLUGIN_SPEC=<empty> niu plugin sync --bootstrap (exit 0, no output, no network), and a niu --version check against this tag and the engine revision.

Integrity (niubash#311): SHA256SUMS lists the SHA-256 of every shipped artifact, and each Windows installer has a .sha256 sidecar beside it. niu --self-update verifies the installer against the sidecar before running it and aborts rather than execute anything it cannot verify. The same digests feed the winget manifest InstallerSha256.

Licenses & Source

Niubash itself is MIT (LICENSE in this repository and at the root of every package). The Windows packages bundle third-party payloads pre-installed from scripts/release/preinstall.json; each component ships its canonical license text under licenses\<component>\ in the package tree, and the installer wizard surfaces niubash's own license at install time:

  • gawk — GPL-3.0-or-later; upstream & Corresponding Source: https://www.gnu.org/software/gawk/ (GNU gawk 5.4.1; the payload's DLL closure libreadline8/libncurses6/libmpfr-6/libgmp-10 is GPL-covered and ships under the same license). Provenance sheet: scripts/release/licenses/gawk/PROVENANCE.md (staged as licenses\gawk\ in every Windows package).
  • niugit — GPL-2.0-only; upstream & Corresponding Source: https://github.com/git-for-windows/git — Corresponding Source for this build: the Git for Windows v2.56.0.windows.4 source tarball, rebuilt by unixwin/niu-git (patches/ + scripts/build.py recipe: https://github.com/unixwin/niu-git). Provenance sheet: scripts/release/licenses/niugit/PROVENANCE.md (staged as licenses\niugit\ in every Windows package).
  • ripgrep — MIT OR Unlicense; upstream & Corresponding Source: https://github.com/BurntSushi/ripgrep. Provenance sheet: scripts/release/licenses/ripgrep/PROVENANCE.md (staged as licenses\ripgrep\ in every Windows package).
  • fd — MIT OR Apache-2.0; upstream & Corresponding Source: https://github.com/sharkdp/fd. Provenance sheet: scripts/release/licenses/fd/PROVENANCE.md (staged as licenses\fd\ in every Windows package).

The pre-install caveat above applies per leg: a build that shipped without a given entry (no matching artifact for that architecture) also shipped without that entry's payload — the component and its license directory are simply absent from that package.

deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness ↗ 1 release · 1 prerelease
dsh-v0.2.2-alpha.3v0.2.2-alpha.3 Prerelease2026-11-03T07:56:48Z

中文 | English

<h3 id="cn-v0.2.2-alpha.3-5">新增功能</h3>

  • 支持统一右键与键盘菜单，覆盖会话、工作区和侧边栏，并支持开发者扩展 @std4453
  • 文件预览支持常驻目录树和文件定位功能。 @yudshj
  • 实验性「变更请求与工作树」统一代码审查与工作树入口，支持通过可搜索文件树和逐文件导航查看本地及 PR 差异，将选中代码和评论交给 Agent，并跟踪检查与评审状态。 @LegGasai

体验优化

  • 改善手机端侧栏导航与窄屏布局，保持会话标题稳定，并让模型目录输入和插件发现页操作完整可见。 @Stevring @Saafo @EricMoin
  • 菜单和浮窗优先保持在可见区域内，跟随浏览器缩放、平移和软键盘变化。 @Stevring
  • Trajectory 支持独立折叠子调用，保留父调用和摘要；搜索或跨视图定位时可展开对应子调用。 @kermanx
  • 优化对话中思考内容的展示，摘要、展开正文和翻译不再显示模型输出开头的签名前缀。 @LegGasai
  • 减少会话状态更新时的重复发布和目录处理开销，并降低长回复流式组装的运行时成本。 @liukx0205 @imccyu
  • 改善底部输入框的布局稳定性，减少输入、菜单展开和统计更新时的跳动。 @imccyu

问题修复

  • 通过 pi-ai 接入的模型在会话图片超过请求数量上限时，自动省略最早的图片并继续请求，避免持续失败。 @turtle2099
  • 修复 OpenCode 路由下自动审核在工具执行前失败的问题，审查请求携带对应会话的身份和用途。 @LegGasai
  • 修复 alpha.2 引入的空会话恢复后被当作「未命名」会话持续显示的问题，并恢复按最后提示时间排序。 @imccyu
  • 修复包含旧版子代理描述的历史会话无法打开，并导致会话搜索失败的问题。 @turtle2099
  • 修复加载更早的已压缩会话历史后，聊天记录停止加载和更新的问题。 @turtle2099
  • 聊天中的 Markdown 文件链接容忍无效行号，并支持行范围及行列后缀；无法定位时仍打开原文件，URL 保持为网页链接。 @kermanx
  • 文本和 Markdown 文件预览在刷新后保留阅读位置，文件暂时变短时也保留原位置，避免跳回第一页。 @yudshj
  • Trajectory 保留失败工具实际返回的文本，列表、详情和复制不再用合成错误信息替换它；原生工具和嵌套 PTC 调用使用相同规则。 @kermanx
  • 修复部分历史 run_code 记录的代码页缺少 TypeScript 或 Python 语法高亮的问题。 @kermanx

其他变更

  • 同步 Cordis Core 至 4.0.0-rc.11、Loader 至 1.0.0-rc.8、Include 至 1.1.1，统一配置访问、条目变更和持久化行为。 @shigma @imccyu
  • pi-ai 支持插件按会话和请求用途提供动态请求头；无效请求头在请求发出前报错。 @LegGasai
  • 插件可为自己的设置页面提供导航图标，卸载后恢复默认图标。 @Magolor
  • 插件安装失败时显示完整诊断与 Host 日志入口，并区分安装失败和安装后启用失败。 @kermanx
  • 「Git 工作树」入口已合入「变更请求与工作树」，此前启用独立插件的用户需手动从 profile 的 dsh.profile.bundles 中移除 @deepseek-ai/dsh-experimental-tool-worktree
  • 「自动化任务」改为统一的宿主级管理，支持手动创建、直接编辑和通过预填提示在会话中创建，保留编辑草稿并支持冲突复核，可选择复用或新建会话执行任务。 @ZiyaZhang @Magolor
  • 新增默认关闭的 apply_patch 工具，一次完成多文件新增、编辑、删除和移动，并在 Review 中查看变更。 @sjw1231 @CreatixChu
  • TypeScript 与 Python SDK 补齐原生命令和插件管理能力，支持计划审阅、目标续跑与操作审批；插件变更重启后生效。 @LogosVeritas @koalazf99
  • macOS Computer Use 改用独立应用申请系统权限，打开对应系统设置并提供应用拖入引导，返回后刷新权限状态。 @CreatixChu
  • 文件修改的先读和外部修改防护默认关闭，沙箱限制和 Review 保持不变；需要原防护的 profile 可显式恢复观察策略。 @sjw1231
  • 回退 alpha.2 中引入的跨行 $$ 数学块的不稳定兼容解析。 @yudshj

<h3 id="en-v0.2.2-alpha.3-5">New Features</h3>

  • Unify mouse and keyboard context menus across Sessions, workspaces, and the sidebar, with support for developer extensions. by @std4453
  • Keep a directory tree in file previews and locate the current file. by @yudshj
  • Unify code review and worktree access in the experimental change-request and worktree feature. Browse local and PR diffs through a searchable file tree and per-file navigation, quote selected code and comments to the Agent, and track checks and reviews. by @LegGasai

Improvements

  • Improve mobile sidebar navigation and narrow-screen layouts, keeping Session titles stable and model-catalog inputs and plugin-discovery controls fully visible. by @Stevring, @Saafo, @EricMoin
  • Keep menus and floating panels within the visible viewport where possible, following browser zooming, panning, and on-screen keyboard changes. by @Stevring
  • Fold Trajectory subcalls independently while keeping their parent calls and summaries visible. Reveal matching subcalls through search or cross-view navigation. by @kermanx
  • Improve reasoning presentation in conversations by hiding leading signature metadata from summaries, expanded content, and translations. by @LegGasai
  • Reduce repeated publications and catalog processing during Session state updates, and lower runtime overhead when assembling streaming replies. by @liukx0205, @imccyu
  • Improve the layout stability of the docked composer, reducing movement during typing, menu expansion, and statistics updates. by @imccyu

Bug Fixes

  • Continue pi-ai conversations that exceed the request image-count limit by offloading the oldest images, preventing repeated failures. by @turtle2099
  • Fix Auto review failures before tool execution on OpenCode routes by supplying the reviewed Session identity and request purpose. by @LegGasai
  • Fix a regression introduced in alpha.2 that leaves resumed empty Sessions visible as untitled Sessions, and restore sorting by the last prompt time. by @imccyu
  • Restore opening historical Sessions with older subagent descriptors and fix the resulting Session search failures. by @turtle2099
  • Fix chat history loading and live updates getting stuck after loading earlier compacted history. by @turtle2099
  • Accept line ranges and line/column suffixes in Markdown file links, open the file when a location is invalid, and retain URLs as web links. by @kermanx
  • Retain reading position in text and Markdown previews across refreshes and temporary file shortening instead of jumping back to the first page. by @yudshj
  • Preserve the actual text returned by failed tools in Trajectory lists, details, and copied output, including native tools and nested PTC calls. by @kermanx
  • Restore TypeScript and Python syntax highlighting for affected historical run_code records. by @kermanx
  • Show the appropriate date, time, interval, and weekday controls when opening existing scheduled tasks, with field-specific correction guidance. Preserve timing rules when only the name or instruction changes, and retain original text for complex rules or values beyond control precision. by @Magolor

Chores

  • Update Cordis Core to 4.0.0-rc.11, Loader to 1.0.0-rc.8, and Include to 1.1.1, unifying configuration access, entry changes, and persistence behavior. by @shigma, @imccyu
  • Allow plugins to supply dynamic pi-ai headers using Session identity and request purpose, rejecting invalid headers before dispatch. by @LegGasai
  • Allow plugins to supply navigation icons for their settings pages, restoring the default icon when unloaded. by @Magolor
  • Show complete diagnostics and the Host log location for plugin failures, distinguishing installation failures from subsequent activation failures. by @kermanx
  • The Git worktree entry is now included in change requests and worktrees. Users who enabled the standalone plugin must manually remove @deepseek-ai/dsh-experimental-tool-worktree from dsh.profile.bundles in their profile.
  • Unify scheduled-task management across the Host with manual creation, direct editing, and prefilled prompts for creation in a Session. Retain drafts, support conflict review, and run tasks in reusable or new Sessions. by @ZiyaZhang, @Magolor
  • Add apply_patch, disabled by default, to create, edit, delete, and move multiple files in one call, with changes available in Review. by @sjw1231, @CreatixChu
  • Extend the TypeScript and Python SDKs with native commands and plugin management, supporting plan review, goal continuation, and operation approvals. Restart the SDK to apply plugin changes. by @LogosVeritas, @koalazf99
  • Request macOS Computer Use permissions through a standalone app, opening the relevant system settings with drag-in guidance and refreshing permission status upon return. by @CreatixChu
  • Disable read-before-write and external-change checks by default while retaining sandbox limits and Review. Profiles requiring the previous checks can explicitly restore the observation policy. by @sjw1231
  • Revert the unstable compatibility parsing for multiline $$ display math introduced in alpha.2. by @yudshj

Full Changelog: https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/compare/dsh-v0.2.2-alpha.2...dsh-v0.2.2-alpha.3

upstash/context7 ↗ 3 releases · 3 stable

MCP Bundle for @upstash/context7-mcp@4.5.0

View release →

@upstash/context7-mcp@4.5.0 Stable2026-11-03T06:41:38Z

Minor Changes

  • 7836c58: Research preview: a submit-docs-feedback tool sends feedback on the documentation that query-docs returned: a score from 0 to 5, what was wrong, and a short comment. It is listed only with research preview on: --research-preview or CONTEXT7_RESEARCH_PREVIEW=1 for stdio, and ?research_preview=1 on the MCP URL for the hosted server. With research preview on, the server instructions also ask the agent to send this feedback after it answers. API calls carry X-Context7-Research-Preview: 1, so Context7 can count how many people use the research preview.

Patch Changes

  • 5f41ea5: Record whether an OAuth client uses a Client ID Metadata Document or a registered client ID in MCP authentication telemetry.
  • c964733: In stdio mode, an API key that is an unset variable placeholder (${CONTEXT7_API_KEY} or $CONTEXT7_API_KEY) now counts as no key: the server falls back to CONTEXT7_API_KEY, and when that is not usable either it logs one warning and runs without a key. Before, it sent the placeholder as the key and every tool call failed with 401. Gemini CLI passes the placeholder through when the user skips the Context7 extension's API key prompt. API keys are now trimmed, so a key with surrounding spaces is sent without them. The HTTP transport is unchanged.

View release →

ctx7@0.9.0 Stable2026-11-03T06:41:35Z

Minor Changes

  • 60e59f9: Add ctx7 search <query> to search documentation across all libraries in one step, without a library ID. Optional --library, --library-version and --language narrow the results, and --json returns structured output.

Patch Changes

  • ceaf5f9: Write the OpenCode MCP entry inside mcp.servers when the config already uses the OpenCode 2.x layout, without the 1.x enabled field. ctx7 setup also moves an entry that an earlier version wrote next to mcp.servers. Other configs keep the 1.x layout, which OpenCode 1.x and 2.x both read. ctx7 remove finds Context7 in either layout.
  • 35bd973: Show the update notice to canary users once the matching stable version is released.

neovim/neovim ↗ 1 release · 1 prerelease
nightlyNvim development (prerelease) build Prerelease2026-11-03T05:17:36Z 
NVIM v0.13.0-dev-2009+g6332ba64fe
Build type: RelWithDebInfo
LuaJIT 2.1.1793383622

Release notes

  • Changelog (fixes + features)
  • News (:help news in Nvim)

Install

Windows

Zip

  1. Download nvim-win64.zip (or nvim-win-arm64.zip for ARM)
  2. Extract the zip
  3. Run nvim.exe in your terminal

MSI

  1. Download nvim-win64.msi (or nvim-win-arm64.msi for ARM)
  2. Run the MSI
  3. Run nvim.exe in your terminal

Note: On Windows "Server" you may need to install vcruntime*.dll.

macOS (x86_64)

  1. Download nvim-macos-x86_64.tar.gz
  2. Run xattr -c ./nvim-macos-x86_64.tar.gz (to avoid "unknown developer" warning)
  3. Extract: tar xzvf nvim-macos-x86_64.tar.gz
  4. Run ./nvim-macos-x86_64/bin/nvim

macOS (arm64)

  1. Download nvim-macos-arm64.tar.gz
  2. Run xattr -c ./nvim-macos-arm64.tar.gz (to avoid "unknown developer" warning)
  3. Extract: tar xzvf nvim-macos-arm64.tar.gz
  4. Run ./nvim-macos-arm64/bin/nvim

Linux (x86_64)

If your system does not have the required glibc version, try the (unsupported) builds for older glibc.

AppImage

  1. Download nvim-linux-x86_64.appimage
  2. Run chmod u+x nvim-linux-x86_64.appimage && ./nvim-linux-x86_64.appimage
    • If your system does not have FUSE you can extract the appimage:
      ./nvim-linux-x86_64.appimage --appimage-extract
./squashfs-root/usr/bin/nvim

Tarball

  1. Download nvim-linux-x86_64.tar.gz
  2. Extract: tar xzvf nvim-linux-x86_64.tar.gz
  3. Run ./nvim-linux-x86_64/bin/nvim

Linux (arm64)

AppImage

  1. Download nvim-linux-arm64.appimage
  2. Run chmod u+x nvim-linux-arm64.appimage && ./nvim-linux-arm64.appimage
    • If your system does not have FUSE you can extract the appimage:
      ./nvim-linux-arm64.appimage --appimage-extract
./squashfs-root/usr/bin/nvim

Tarball

  1. Download nvim-linux-arm64.tar.gz
  2. Extract: tar xzvf nvim-linux-arm64.tar.gz
  3. Run ./nvim-linux-arm64/bin/nvim

Other

  • Install by package manager

View release →

stablyai/orca ↗ 1 release · 1 stable
v1.4.247 Stable2026-11-03T03:36:43Z

Improved Chat UI, richer phone reviews & more ways to make Orca yours ✨

  • 🔄 Continue after usage limits reset: An optional setting resumes your chat automatically when its usage limit resets.
  • 📱 Richer reviews on your phone: Browse branch commits, jump between files in diffs, and read or add pull-request review comments alongside the code.
  • 🎨 Make Orca yours: Discover more themes, preview fonts in their own typefaces, and import Ghostty or Kitty configuration files.
  • 🧩 More powerful plugins: Build custom editors, live status items and panels, and connect plugins to agent prompts, launches and workspace actions.
  • 🤝 Clearer agent activity: Running workers stay visible after their coordinator finishes, with easier account switching and clearer hand-off cards.
  • ⚡ Smoother everyday work: Fewer unnecessary redraws and offscreen animations, more reliable saved replies, and fixes for workspaces, terminals and emulator previews.
  • 💬 Improved Chat UI: Choose Chat or CLI for each agent in Chat UI settings, keep drafts when continuing from a terminal, and see your selected permission mode clearly.

Highlights

  • Chat UI: Choose Chat or CLI for each agent directly in Chat UI settings. Permission choices stay visible, model and effort controls remain usable while options refresh, and focusing the composer keeps open overlays in place.
  • Conversation continuity: Keep unsent text when moving from a terminal into chat, and preserve received replies and tool results while saving is busy. Turn on automatic continuation to resume a chat after its usage limit resets.
  • Phone source control: Review each branch commit on its own page, jump between files, fold diff context, and read or add review comments alongside the code. The pull-request tab focuses on what blocks the merge, with clearer next actions and automatic refreshes.
  • Themes & fonts: Find GitHub, VS Code and catalog themes in the appearance controls. Preview each font in its own typeface, import Ghostty themes with light/dark pairs, and bring in Kitty configuration files too.
  • Plugins: Create custom editors that keep pending edits through saves and reloads, live status bar items, and component panels with a Box layout container. Hooks connect plugins to agent launches, prompts, tool calls, terminal sends and workspace creation; screenshot and testing tools help check the result.
  • Agents & orchestration: Running workers remain in the Agents list after their coordinator finishes. Hand-off cards make delegated work easier to follow, and Codex and Claude account switchers are simpler to use.
  • Remote work & workspaces: Preview remote HTML outside registered workspaces, find projects in stopped WSL distros after a restart, and see clearer Git progress. Child worktrees stay in the caller’s project, folder roots remain visible, and branch links open the current pull request.
  • Responsiveness & reliability: Offscreen animations pause, unrelated settings changes cause fewer diff redraws, and unused editor models are released. Terminal fixes prevent stray escape text, while emulator previews offer clearer connection and startup feedback.

Compatibility & upgrade notes

  • Chat or CLI per agent: Chat UI is out of Experimental. Shared launch controls are also available in Settings → Chat UI, so you can choose which surface each agent opens.
  • Automatic continuation is optional: Enable it if you want a usage-limited chat to continue automatically after the limit resets.
  • Phone Send behavior: The phone follows the paired desktop’s send setting. Update Orca Mobile as well as your desktop or server to get the mobile changes.
  • Orchestration panel: The experimental Orchestration panel is disabled while its app-wide lag is addressed. Orchestration commands continue to work.

Known issues

Saved chat storage: Chat history can continue to use more disk space as conversations grow; automatic storage compaction is not included in this release.

Plugin command replacements: A plugin replacing a built-in command can still finish after Orca has used the normal command instead, causing the action to happen twice.

astral-sh/uv ↗ 1 release · 1 stable
0.13.7 Stable2026-11-03T02:45:04Z

Release Notes

Released on 2026-11-03.

Preview features

  • Add --output-version to uv workspace metadata for requesting a specific schema version (#22958)
  • Report aliased OSV records as a single vulnerability in uv audit (#22990)

Bug fixes

  • Treat workspace member paths added by uv init or uv add as literals instead of glob patterns (#23008)
  • Prevent uv's Windows trampolines from closing handles they did not inherit (#22977)
  • Allow uvx to run tools with CPython builds whose versions contain local identifiers (#23013)
  • Generate valid module names when uv init creates packaged projects whose names start with a digit (#23022)
  • Preserve comments around existing inline dependency groups when uv add updates a group (#23017)
  • Allow uv remove with an empty inline tool.uv.sources table (#23012)

Documentation

  • Correct the cache-keys inline table example (#22984)

Install uv 0.13.7

Install prebuilt binaries via shell script

curl --proto '=https' --tlsv1.2 -LsSf https://releases.astral.sh/github/uv/releases/download/0.13.7/uv-installer.sh | sh

Install prebuilt binaries via powershell script

powershell -ExecutionPolicy Bypass -c "irm https://releases.astral.sh/github/uv/releases/download/0.13.7/uv-installer.ps1 | iex"

The artifacts in this release have attestations generated with GitHub Artifact Attestations. These can be verified by using the GitHub CLI:

gh attestation verify <file-path of downloaded artifact> --repo astral-sh/uv

You can also download the attestation from GitHub and verify against that directly:

gh attestation verify <file-path of downloaded artifact> --bundle <file-path of downloaded attestation>

can1357/oh-my-pi ↗ 1 release · 1 stable
v18.16.0 Stable2026-11-02T19:49:20Z

@oh-my-pi/pi-agent-core

Changed

  • Deprecated createFileOps, computeFileLists, formatFileOperations, upsertFileOperations, isUrlSchemePath and FileOperations in @oh-my-pi/pi-agent-core/compaction; they now re-export @oh-my-pi/snapcompact, which callers should import directly

Fixed

  • Context estimates for a model without a known tokenizer family (Gemini, Grok and the like), and token counts made without a model (compress metrics, the legacy estimateTokens export), count text in o200k_base instead of guessing 4 bytes per token, which undercounted what those providers bill by 11–48% on code-heavy sessions and let compaction fire late after a switch to them (#17607 by @will-bogusz)
  • Context estimates no longer charge a reader another model's thinking signatures or redacted thinking, which the request strips: a Claude session switched to Grok or Gemini, including Gemini through Factory, counted them as text (#17607 by @will-bogusz)

Removed

  • Removed PI_TOKENIZER_ACCURATE: every model is now counted with a native tokenizer (#17607 by @will-bogusz)

@oh-my-pi/pi-ai

Added

  • /login mistral signs in through the browser like the Mistral Vibe CLI and stores the minted API key, whose usage counts against the plan's Vibe Code quota instead of pay-as-you-go API credits; MISTRAL_API_KEY still works (#13875 by @Gauthier-Huguenin, continued in #17672 by @abilliontokens)
  • Added dropsForeignReasoningPayload(turn, model), which tells whether transformMessages certainly strips a turn's thinking signatures and redacted thinking on the way to model, and, for Factory Droid's Gemini wire, isFactoryDroidGeminiWire(model) and keepsFactoryGeminiReasoning(turn) (#17607 by @will-bogusz)
  • Factory Droid Beam 1.0 requests accept tool-call turns that arrive without reasoning (#17766 by @will-bogusz)

Changed

  • Deprecated formatError on the embeddings, images, rerank, speech, transcriptions, video, pi-native and SystemOne server modules (now aliases of codedErrorResponse / noStoreErrorResponse in auth-gateway/http) and asStrict in providers/aws-sigv4 (now asStrictBytes from @oh-my-pi/pi-utils)

Fixed

  • Fixed Factory Droid Fable requests offering Opus 5 as the refusal fallback when your organization's model policy excludes Opus 5; Claude requests now offer only fallback models your account can use, and a request served by another organization's login than the one your model list came from offers none (#17772 by @will-bogusz)

@oh-my-pi/pi-catalog

Added

  • /login mistral now offers the Mistral Vibe browser sign-in beside MISTRAL_API_KEY (#13875 by @Gauthier-Huguenin, continued in #17672 by @abilliontokens)
  • Added revisionAtLeast to compat/revision and exported withOpenAICompatibleDiscoveryTimeout from discovery/openai-compatible
  • Added the Factory Droid models droid 1.1.0 serves: GPT-6.1 Terra, GPT-6 Sol and Luna Fast Mode, GPT-6 Astra and GPT-6.1 Sol Ultra Fast (after the organization's high-cost opt-in), Nemotron 3.5 Super, Muse Spark Open, Muse Spark 1.2 and Beam 1.0, each listed once the account may use it (#17766 by @will-bogusz)
  • Added Factory Droid's dated credit rates: the credit rate follows Factory's promotions, discounted from a promotion's start and back to the base rate at its end, using the same dated rate cards as dollar prices (currently GPT-5.6 Sol and Gemini 3.7/3.8 Flash) (#16309 by @will-bogusz)

Changed

  • Factory Droid lists Opus 5.5 Fast, GPT-6.1 Sol and MiniMax M3 without the feature flags Factory retired, and no longer routes GPT-5.5 to Snowflake (#17766 by @will-bogusz)
  • Factory Droid Opus 5.5 now falls back to Opus 5 when Anthropic refuses a request, as droid does, while your account can use Opus 5 (#17772 by @will-bogusz)

Fixed

  • GPT-3.5 and GPT-4 (Turbo) resolve the cl100k tokenizer, so their context estimates count in cl100k_base rather than the o200k_base default for models without one (#17607 by @will-bogusz)
  • Added Scaleway Generative APIs as a built-in provider with 12 serverless chat models (glm-5.3 default), API-key login, and live model discovery (#16687 by @reda-maizate)

@oh-my-pi/pi-coding-agent

Added

  • With the Hindsight memory backend, retain and learn can now store a memory with scope: "global" so every project recalls it. It is opt-in through the new hindsight.globalScope setting (off by default) and needs global or per-project-tagged scoping (#17706 by @alphastorm, from #15030 by @kithawk).
  • Added btw.inject (off by default; /settings → Interaction → "/btw Steering Inject"): when you ask /btw for a steering prompt, it drafts one in an <inject> block shown behind a pink rail, and Alt+I (app.btw.inject) sends it to the main session (as a steer while the agent runs, else as a new turn) from the inline answer or the BTW history sheet, once per answer from the current run. Once sent, the block turns green with ➤ on each line and "Injected into main session" below it; if the main session drops or refuses the send, the block turns back to pending and the same key retries (#17618 by @apullin)
  • With OTLP metrics export configured (OTEL_EXPORTER_OTLP_METRICS_ENDPOINT), composer predictions export content-free omp.composer.prediction.* metrics by serving model: requests by outcome (offered, skipped, stale, cancelled, timeout, error) with their duration, predictions shown (counted on the first frame that paints them), shown predictions by whether they were accepted with Tab or Right, and the time from first display to acceptance (#17739 by @wolfiesch)
  • Bash results the output minimizer rewrote now keep which filter ran and the bytes before and after in the session, and the bash card shows them (Minimized: git 120.4KB → 3.2KB) (#16586 by @will-bogusz)
  • Eval completion handles expose attempts (JS await handle.attempts(), Python handle.attempts): each fallback candidate tried, in order, with the model, the reasoning effort sent, its outcome, and the error when it failed (#17782 by @xiangnan0811)
  • Added composer.predictionAlternatives (off by default; /settings → Interaction → "Prediction Alternatives", listed while Composer Predictions is on): the same single prediction request returns up to four different next messages, most likely first. Type a few letters to get ghost text for the first one that matches; after inserting one, press Tab again to pick another from a list. Prediction metrics carry how many alternatives were offered (omp.composer.prediction.choices), and an accepted prediction now resolves when it retires, with the rank of the alternative inserted last (omp.composer.prediction.rank) (#17796 by @wolfiesch)
  • /review and /annotate code-review work from a folder of checkouts: /review owner/repo#123 reviews a GitHub PR from anywhere; /review ./repo [#123 | !7] [focus] reviews in that checkout (#N a GitHub PR, !N a GitLab MR); and outside any repository, the local and open-PR menu choices ask which child repository to use

Changed

  • /resume and omp --resume open at once on your titled sessions (read from ~/.omp/agent/history.db), then fill in prompts, status, and untitled sessions when the full scan finishes; the first scan saves older sessions' titles too, so later opens list them right away
  • With symbolPreset: ascii, status glyphs are three columns wide ([+], [-], [#], [ ]) and status spinners animate as [|] [/] [-] [\] (#17732 by @JustasMasiulis)
  • The subagent HUD marks each running agent with the running status glyph instead of a dot (#17732 by @JustasMasiulis)

Fixed

  • Browser: browser.close({ all: true }) from a subagent no longer closes its parent's tabs; it releases only the calling agent's tabs. Tabs now belong to the agent that opened them rather than its conversation id, so after /new, /resume, fork, or branch they are still frozen, idle-closed, released by close-all, and closed when the session ends instead of leaking (#15975, #16133 by @harshaygadekar)
  • lsp request now sends payload: "null" as JSON null instead of substituting auto-built document params (#17715).
  • A prompt rejected before a turn starts because its model or credential is missing returns to the editor for retry; ./c on an empty conversation reports that there is nothing to resume (#17828).
  • Factory Droid requests keep attached images and snapcompact system-prompt frames when tools carry large runtime state (#17819).
  • pr://<n>, issue://<n>, and repo-less github tool calls in a checkout whose upstream remote is not the fork parent of origin now resolve to origin instead of the upstream project; gh repo set-default still wins
  • omp -p text mode now prints each automatic retry with its error (for example "Couldn't reach api.anthropic.com … check your network") instead of showing only Working... while a turn retries (#17529 by @Mannan-Goyal)
  • omp --export and /export no longer fail with Error: Out of memory on large sessions with subagent transcripts, and use far less memory: the HTML is now streamed to disk instead of built as one string (#17741)
  • A native security_scan with async.enabled on (the default) now waits for its background security-reviewer workers and publishes their findings, instead of ending partial with 0 findings and "The scan session ended without publishing a canonical result" (#17788)
  • Browser (Chrome tabs): a click or drag cancelled before its final release now releases off-page instead of completing on its target, and the page sees no click from it (not even on the document root), a timed-out key combo sends no further key, and a call or run cut off mid-gesture releases the mouse button or keys it held instead of leaving them down in the tab. Input already dispatched to Chrome, including a target release, cannot be recalled and may still take effect after timeout (#17778 by @will-bogusz)
  • Relay mode: tab.waitForSelector, tab.evaluate and tab.goto no longer fail after 20 s when the page or server takes longer; like on a launched Chrome they now run to the call's own timeout, up to omp's 60 s protocol timeout, and a command Chrome never answers names itself and its tab (#17778 by @will-bogusz)
  • Fixed browser tab.click(selector, { button, count }) and frame.click ignoring their options and left-clicking once, so a right-click meant to open a context menu clicked the element instead; cmux tabs refuse buttons and counts they cannot press (#17767 by @will-bogusz)
  • Browser fill in Chrome no longer sometimes puts the new text in front of a Slate editor's old text when the machine is busy, and refuses a rich-text editor whose focus moves to a wrapper or the page instead of reporting success (#17742 by @will-bogusz)
  • Fixed a browser relay call working in a cross-site frame (an embedded sign-in form, say) failing with a bare Target closed when Chrome detached omp from the tab; it now says Chrome detached omp and whether the tab was reattached, as a call in the page itself does (#17768 by @will-bogusz)
  • Browser tabs omp opens with persist: true in your Chrome through the relay now stay open when the session ends (an omp -p run exits, a subagent finishes), so a page left for you is still there; other tabs omp opened still close, and an explicit close racing session cleanup still closes its page (#17751 by @will-bogusz)
  • Fixed keys you type in another app going into a page on macOS when omp's click in a relay tab opened a new tab or popup (#16284 by @will-bogusz)
  • Parked subagents keep their xd:// device mounts when woken after omp restarts, so they no longer lose their earlier Claude thinking (#17746 by @will-bogusz)
  • Fixed cancelling %pip in Python eval leaving the installer process behind in Linux containers and chroots without a usable /proc (#16552 by @will-bogusz)
  • Fixed numbered read pages of files with very long lines ending after a few KB: a line cut to tools.outputMaxColumns now counts the width shown, not its full length, against the page size, so paging no longer stops before such a line or refuses to step past it. When the first cut line is larger than a :raw read returns, the cut notice names that byte cap, and a multi-range read that the page size stops partway through a range says where to continue (#16595 by @will-bogusz)
  • Fixed write accepting the output of a read whose long lines were cut to tools.outputMaxColumns and overwriting the file with the cut lines; it now refuses it as an incomplete read, as it does for other truncated reads (#16595 by @will-bogusz)
  • Fixed a session resumed, or a parked subagent woken, on a later day resending its first message with that day's date, which made Claude drop all of its earlier thinking and missed the prompt cache; earlier messages now keep the date they were sent with (#15343) (#17622 by @will-bogusz)
  • models.yml accepts tokenizer: cl100k on a custom model or override (#17607 by @will-bogusz)
  • Claude no longer receives the reasoning kept in a snapcompact archive whose summary an extension wrote without the reasoning legend, with or without image input, and reads that summary's own words instead of a generic one (#17745 by @will-bogusz)
  • Fixed ~/.omp/agent/agent.db getting corrupted (wiping stored logins) on Linux when grep, read, the bash tool, extensions or eval read it while omp has it open; write and tts now refuse to overwrite a database omp holds open (#15586)

@oh-my-pi/pi-natives

Added

  • Added macOSFrontApp(), which reads the app macOS has in front and can put it back in front for a while (keepInFront) without stopping for the user's typing (#16284 by @will-bogusz)

Fixed

  • Fixed iPhone Mirroring clicks, scrolls and keys reporting success while the mirror showed a connection screen such as "iPhone in Use" instead of the phone; they now fail and quote that screen (#17755 by @will-bogusz)
  • parseKey/matchesKey now recognize the parameterless CSI P/CSI Q/CSI S (and CSI 1 <letter>) forms that kitty-protocol terminals such as Ghostty send for unmodified F1/F2/F4, and modified F2–F9 no longer match other keys (e.g. Ctrl+PageDown as Ctrl+F8) (#17812).
  • Added installSqliteLockShim() (Linux), which moves Bun's SQLite record locks onto open-file-description locks that no other close of the file releases, and heldFileKind(path), which reports a file this process holds open and locked. Both are also on @oh-my-pi/pi-natives/sqlite-locks, which loads the addon on first call (#15586)

@oh-my-pi/snapcompact

Added

  • Added archiveIncludesThinking(), whether a compaction may replay reasoning from its summary's legend or its archive's text, and viewSummary(), which keeps a summary the template did not write in its own words, followed by the template's guide to the view's text (#17745 by @will-bogusz)

Fixed

  • Fixed viewWithoutFrames and viewWithoutThinking keeping an archive's reasoning for a reader without it when the summary (say, one an extension wrote) declares no ¶think: scope, and replacing such a summary with the template's (#17745 by @will-bogusz)

Fixed

@oh-my-pi/pi-tui

Breaking Changes

  • buildExecutionFrame sets its loader into the new bottomBorder itself, so callers no longer add it as a row and must dispose it; buildStatusFooter no longer takes status or exitCode, and executionStatusLabel returns a settled run's exit N / cancelled marker for that border (#17732 by @JustasMasiulis)
  • SessionSelectorOptions.loadAllSessions and SessionPickerHost.loadAllSessions resolve to a SessionListing, and allSessions takes one, so the all-projects scope can show preliminary rows before its complete listing
  • Editor.prediction is replaced by Editor.predictions, which returns every prediction for the next prompt, most likely first, and Editor.onPredictionAccepted receives the inserted prediction's index in that list (#17796 by @wolfiesch)

Added

  • SessionSelectorOptions.completeSessions (and SessionPickerOptions.completeSessions) replaces the picker's preliminary rows with the complete listing once it settles, keeping the query, the selected session, and deletes/renames made meanwhile
  • /btw answers can show a steering prompt as a block behind a pink rail (a toned card in Tern) that Alt+I (app.btw.inject, remappable) sends to the main session; once sent it turns green with ➤ on each line, without moving (#17618 by @apullin)
  • Added singleLine and finiteWidth to utils for row-confined text and cell widths
  • Loader.inlineText() returns the current spinner frame and message as one styled line, and DynamicBorder.setLabel() sets a label (or a function re-read every render) into the rule (#17732 by @JustasMasiulis)

Changed

  • The ASCII symbol preset's status glyphs are all three columns wide: success is [+], error [-], aborted [#] and pending [ ] (alongside [~] running, [!] warning and [i] info), and status spinners animate as [|] [/] [-] [\] (#17732 by @JustasMasiulis)
  • Built-in renderers no longer use status.done, theme.status.done or the done value of ToolUIStatus; they remain as aliases of status.success / success (an explicit symbols.overrides["status.done"] still applies to theme.status.done) (#17732 by @JustasMasiulis)
  • Every finished state shows the success glyph (✔, ASCII [+]). Task rows, verdicts and headers, background-job, launch and supervised-process rows, Eval cells, Vibe, Wait and process job rows, Yield and the generic tool card showed a separate • done dot, and a finished Read showed ● (ASCII [x]). Live Task agent rows show the pending or running glyph, and a Task whose merge failed shows the warning glyph (#17732 by @JustasMasiulis)
  • Edit and Write animate their streaming spinner in the card header, as Eval does, and show their preview / streaming label in the card's bottom border instead of on a row below the content; streaming ends once the content has fully arrived (#17732 by @JustasMasiulis)
  • ! shell and $ Python runs show their spinner, and then exit N or cancelled, in the frame's bottom border instead of on rows of their own; the spinner is the status spinner tool headers use (#17732 by @JustasMasiulis)
  • GitHub run-watch job rows use the shared status glyphs: a running job shows the running glyph instead of a warning-colored dot, a queued job the pending glyph instead of a hollow circle (a job in a state omp does not recognize keeps the circle), and a passed job's glyph is green like other success glyphs. Ask's multi-line custom answer lines up under its first line in every symbol preset (#17732 by @JustasMasiulis)

Fixed

  • An LSP result other than diagnostics, such as a raw request response, that contains the error glyph (✘, ASCII [-]) is shown as a response instead of as diagnostics reading "No issues" with the rest of its lines dropped (#17732 by @JustasMasiulis)
  • Editor.onPredictionAccepted and Editor.onPredictionShown report when Tab or Right inserts the rest of the composer prediction and when a frame paints it (at least one cell in the terminal, or the ghost on a native editor node) (#17739 by @wolfiesch)
  • Added OutputMeta.minimized (bash minimizer filter, raw and minimized byte counts, raw-output artifact), shown as a Minimized fact on bash result cards (#16586 by @will-bogusz)
  • Added isColumnTruncationNotice and hasColumnTruncationClause to tools/output-meta, matching the [Some lines truncated to …] clause that formatOutputNotice emits alone or inside a longer notice, and LimitsMeta.columnTruncated.rawByteCap, which makes that notice name the byte cap a :raw read of the first cut line stops at (#16595 by @will-bogusz)
  • Fixed the model browser showing Factory Droid's undiscounted credit rate during a Factory promotion (2× instead of 1.6× for GPT-5.6 Sol); rows now show the rate in force and the model details show the next rate and its date (#16309 by @will-bogusz)
  • With two or more Editor.predictions, the ghost text follows the first one the draft is a prefix of; a typed draft that equals a shorter one keeps the ghost of a longer one. Once the buffer equals one of them with no ghost (always right after Tab, Right, or the list inserts one), a dim ⇥ N more hint appears; press Tab to list them all, then Enter, Tab or Right to swap the whole line (never submitting) or Esc to keep it (#17796 by @wolfiesch)
  • F2 now opens the rename dialog in the resume picker in Ghostty, kitty and other terminals using the kitty keyboard protocol; F1 and F4 shortcuts work there too (#17812).
  • Added Component.nativeTrailers(), which lets a transcript block add native blocks of its own directly after it. Trailing blocks stay visible while the block is collapsed.

Graphify-Labs/graphify ↗ 1 release · 1 stable
v0.9.101graphify 0.9.101 Stable2026-11-02T19:38:56Z

graphify 0.9.101

Resolution-accuracy and cache-correctness release across C++, Rust, Python, Go, the build write guard, and incremental rebuilds.

C++

  • A member call x.m() binds by the receiver's type, now typed from function parameters and class fields and not only locals (void render(Base* b){ b->draw(); } resolves to Base::draw); an untyped or ambiguous receiver keeps its in-file binding (#3215, #4946).
  • An out-of-line or split-header definition attaches to the unique owner whose class file is in its include set, instead of cross-binding to an unrelated same-named function; ambiguous owners fail closed (#4916, #4920).
  • A local callable value (a lambda or std::function) is kept out of global call resolution by byte-range binding lifetimes, so it no longer mints a spurious INFERRED call to a same-named global; a later legitimate global call survives (#4917, #4921).
  • A type-alias reference (using Foo = Bar; then Foo x;) binds by exact spelling and a salted alias keeps its own contains edge (#4951).

Rust

  • A module-qualified call (crate::b::helper(), super::m::f()) resolves through qualifier-verified cross-file resolution, staying inside the caller's crate (#2135, #4944).
  • A call written inside an expression macro's arguments (println!("{}", f()), vec![g()]) is linked, gated to an allowlist so macro_rules! bodies and custom DSLs are left alone (#4945).

Python / Go

  • A bare call inside a method no longer self-binds to a same-named method; it resolves to the imported or module-level function it names (#4933).
  • Imports resolve across PEP 420 namespace packages split over multiple src/ roots (#4929).
  • A Go method value reached through a receiver field is linked as an indirect_call (#1566, #4931).
  • A Go package-level var initialiser references the types it names (#2360, #4932).

Build / cache / rebuild / watch

  • The write guard no longer refuses a healthy graph when entity dedup folds a saved node into a surviving twin; a genuine loss is still refused (#4940, #4952).
  • Independently sourced semantic file evidence survives a fold into an AST file-self node; ordinary INFERRED mention ghosts still fold so graphs do not accumulate duplicates (#4896, #4927).
  • The file-stem alias index only rebinds an edge when the edge's file and the alias target share a language family (#4949).
  • Cache keys keep their written case so README.md and readme.md no longer collide, and hashed/word-count files are opened through os_long_path (#4925, #4937).
  • A --code-only graph stays code-only across hook, update and watch rebuilds (#4923, #4939).
  • A rebuild no longer waits forever when msvcrt.locking is unsupported (#4924, #4926).
  • A full reconcile evicts source records for an excluded linked git worktree (#4919).
  • AST-only updates preserve cross-file semantic evidence whose node id collides with a code definition (#4950).
  • A sliced file is cached only once every slice has succeeded and is assembled in chunk order (#4943, #4947).

The AST cache schema is bumped to 36; warm caches re-run the affected extractions.

Thanks to @nothariharan, @Deepjyoti-Sarmah, @azizur100389, @andrebrait, @ayushcodes10, @madhusudanpatnaik, @Ethan-Ethan-Ethan and @AndresDiagoM.

thedotmack/claude-mem ↗ 1 release · 1 stable
v15.9.0 Stable2026-11-02T18:03:43Z

What's new

Devin support. npx claude-mem install can now set up Devin CLI and Devin Desktop: hooks are written for you, Devin's tool names are understood, and the memory search tools are registered. (#5172, #5276 by @patrickballard and @ssthormess)

My Work refresh on your terms. My Work and Past Focus have a "Refresh now" button (#5249 by @Kirat0201). New settings choose the refresh model per provider: CLAUDE_MEM_WORK_VIEW_CLAUDE_MODEL, CLAUDE_MEM_WORK_VIEW_CODEX_MODEL and CLAUDE_MEM_WORK_VIEW_GEMINI_MODEL (#5236 by @LucianVane).

Remote workers. New CLAUDE_MEM_WORKER_URL setting. A bad value fails with a clear error instead of falling back, and any worker that isn't on your own machine must use https. (#5285 by @Kirat0201)

Privacy and security

  • Turning off error reporting now also stops errors already saved on disk from being sent. (#5272 by @thedotmack)
  • More secrets are redacted before they are stored: Slack and Discord webhook URLs, Stripe webhook secrets, PyPI, SendGrid, DigitalOcean, Shopify, Docker Hub and GitLab tokens, Basic credentials after Authorization headers, and CLI flags like --auth-token and --client-secret. (#5277, #5207, #5204, #5224, #5222, #5197 by @kevin9327; #5205 by @parveen0029)
  • Other websites running on localhost can no longer send writes to the worker through the work-state, dedup, corpus and advisor routes. (#5200 by @DeryFerd)
  • The Kimi config backup is now readable only by you. (#5290 by @patrickballard)

Fixes you may notice

Memory and search

  • Switching observer providers mid-conversation no longer duplicates observations. (#5227 by @liuyinchu)
  • Repeated turn summaries no longer pile up. (#5228 by @Dev-0710)
  • The transcript watcher no longer loses an end-of-session summary it could not queue. (#5246 by @liuyinchu)
  • File searches match whichever way the path separators are written, src/a.ts or src\a.ts. (#5214 by @rudycelekli)
  • All-digit dateStart and dateEnd search values are read as epoch milliseconds. (#5234 by @vinirib)
  • Restarting the worker during a Chroma rebuild no longer embeds finished documents again. (#5238 by @vinirib)
  • Corpora built for merged projects keep the adopted memories, and a failed rebuild no longer loses the saved corpus. (#5235 by @liuyinchu; #5291 by @rudycelekli)
  • Observations with a stray closing tag on the type are still read, and batches survive verification holds. (#5221 by @Montinou; #5252 by @Kirat0201)
  • Context refresh keeps marker examples you wrote yourself, and CLAUDE.md generation checks duplicate path segments only from the project folder down. (#5203 by @rudycelekli; #5232 by @vinirib)

My Work and viewer

  • Reviews a few characters over a limit no longer fail the whole refresh, and oversized reviews recover after one retry. (#5230 by @juelpb; #5286 by @Kirat0201)
  • My Work no longer slows other worker requests on large databases, and saving rules or focus rebuilds the view once instead of twice. (#5284 by @Kirat0201; #5239 by @LucianVane)
  • Viewer filters keep case-variant sessions in project filters and selected categories at zero count. (#5213, #5215 by @rudycelekli)

Importing history and token usage

  • Claude transcripts with whitespace in their JSON are no longer skipped. (#5289 by @rudycelekli)
  • Codex and Gemini prompts are no longer imported twice, and legacy Gemini usage is no longer counted twice across migrated copies. (#5292, #5217, #5295 by @rudycelekli)
  • Token usage stays partial instead of failing when one transcript source can't be read. (#5294 by @rudycelekli)

Providers and accounts

  • Worker recycles are no longer reported as authentication failures. (#5278 by @2023Anita)
  • macOS: the worker retries the login keychain directly and gives better diagnostics when a credential lookup fails. This may not fix every case in #5250. (#5258 by @Kirat0201)
  • OpenRouter: endpoints that require reasoning no longer lose the batch; the request is resent once without the setting. (#5267 by @markjoin123)
  • A rejected overage bucket on a request that was served is no longer treated as a refusal. (#5223 by @beckman55)

Install and CLI

  • npx claude-mem adopt --help and npx claude-mem cleanup --help now print help. Before, they ran the command. Not fixed yet: the claude-mem launcher on your PATH still runs them, so use npx to read their help. (#5288 by @WhitePepperLambSoup)
  • macOS 27: a launcher the OS killed is rebuilt instead of being kept as foreign. (#5253 by @REI-330)
  • Windows: launcher installs retry short file locks, and npm's claude.exe is used instead of the sh shim. (#5256 by @HoneyTyagii; #5198 by @balabommablock-cpu)
  • Codex: CODEX_HOME is respected, and quoted TOML settings are updated correctly. (#5216, #5212 by @rudycelekli)
  • T3 Code: default Claude and Codex providers are found even when T3 never saved them. (#5283 by @balabommablock-cpu)
  • OpenCode: expected registry-only setup calls no longer warn. (#5264 by @Bl0ck154)
  • Hermes: the cached project key for a checkout now expires, so it can't go stale. (#5287 by @DeryFerd)
  • claude-md:regenerate reads the database from CLAUDE_MEM_DATA_DIR. (#5210 by @Bortlesboat)

For developers and hosted users

  • Server-mode hooks send contentSessionId on semantic injection. (#5281 by @rich-murphey)
  • Claude observer per-turn token usage is now logged. (#5293 by @Kirat0201)
  • Claude Duet cloud sessions that can't reach cmem.ai are pointed at the Claude-Mem connector. (#5243 by @thedotmack)
  • CI and test improvements. (#5201, #5202 by @DeryFerd; #5254, #5257 by @xunguangzlj-cloud)
  • Chinese README terminology updates. (#5208 by @SaigyoujYuyuko)

Thank you

This release is mostly community work. Release Date: November 2, 2026

Patch release. This tag rolls up the ~776 PRs merged since v0.22.5 into a stable release for the GitHub release (CLI and installers), the Microsoft Store app, Docker and Hermes Cloud. Full curated notes for this window ship with v0.23.0.

About this release

Measured at commit f0d94882e89, the window since v0.22.5 contains 3,448 non-merge commits across 4,316 changed files (+248,579 / −94,077), 776 merged PRs and 385 closed issues.

Also in the window, undocumented here on purpose: the Webapp served from the gateway's own listener (hermes webapp, a Desktop Web Access pane to open it on your network or your own URL behind Nous sign-in, and Desktop plugins loading in a browser tab), /model reset to clear a session's model pin without losing history, per-skill locks against autonomous writes (lockable from the Desktop Capabilities pane), opt-in prepared compaction (compression.prepare_ahead), hermes cron cancel plus a per-job missed-run policy and a Stop button in the Desktop cron editor, /delegation choices saved for the next session, hermes migrate and setup moving Grok Bot and OpenBot users over, opt-in OS-keychain storage for the vault key, the catalog's requires_hermes enforced at plugin install and update, agent-picked ElevenLabs voices in text_to_speech, a pre_llm_call hook that can block a turn before inference, Desktop group chat rename, disband, mentions and attachment paste, every profile shown as a Desktop sidebar section, Telegram video notes, Slack emoji reactions in thread context, and resumable downloads in the package manager.

Full curated release notes for this window ship with v0.23.0, which will document everything from v0.22.0 onward — highlights, feature areas, and complete contributor credits. Nothing in this window is skipped.

Updating

  • Microsoft Store app: updates through the Store.

microsoft/skill-recorder ↗ 1 release · 1 stable
v0.8.0Skill Recorder v0.8.0 Stable2026-11-02T16:49:16Z

Source-only release

Skill Recorder 0.8.0 is published from commit 505c97ae47be5642c0e1b44ebb8d1c1530c298fa.

This release is source-only. No installers or portable binaries are attached. GitHub's generated archives contain the tagged repository source only.

Changes

  • Import an existing recording: use the smaller folder button beside Record to select a local MP4 or WebM, such as a downloaded Teams demonstration.
  • Check actual video and audio decoding before proceeding. Unsupported codecs and invalid media are rejected with an explanation; no converter, standalone FFmpeg installation, or new application dependency is required.
  • Add optional details to explain the task, supplement the demonstration, or suggest where it appears. Leaving details empty is supported. Timestamps guide the AI; they do not trim the recording.
  • Transcribe audio locally with the existing Whisper model, then reconstruct activities from the video, speech, and user details. Silent videos and videos without audio are supported; transcription failures stop analysis so they can be retried.
  • Reuse the existing activity-review and skill-building flow: edit, reorder, add or remove activities, then create, review, and install/export a skill.
  • Preserve live screen recording, its settings, and its event-first analysis behavior.
  • Keep the original recording untouched. Store a local session copy with cancellation, retry, and privacy protection for outgoing frames and text.

Compatibility and limitations

  • Import limits: 512 MiB, 15 minutes, and up to 3840 x 2160 pixels (or an equivalent pixel count). Audio must be mono or stereo at no more than 48 kHz. One video track and at most one audio track are supported.
  • MP4/WebM is a container, not a guarantee of codec support: the shipped Chromium runtime must be able to decode the selected file. Chromium uses the already-shipped, reviewed codec library; no separate FFmpeg executable is added.
  • CPU-based transcription can take several minutes for longer recordings. The current status does not show per-chunk progress, and canceling during inference may wait for a processing boundary.
  • A conservative synthetic 512 MiB / 15-minute stereo stress case with Whisper resident peaked at approximately 4.6 GB (4.25 GiB) across Electron processes on Windows ARM64. This is not a universal memory ceiling and excludes other apps and the external Copilot CLI process. The import decoder has a 2 GiB working-set guard.
  • Imported video lacks the OS events captured during live recording. Hidden clipboard contents, unreadable commands, and actions neither shown nor spoken cannot be recovered reliably; review the inferred activities before creating a skill.
  • Selecting a file does not upload the recording. Choosing Analyze sends selected frames, transcribed speech, and additional details to GitHub Copilot through the existing protection pipeline. Only import recordings you are authorized to use; protection is a safety net, not a guarantee.
  • Dependencies and installer scripts are unchanged. Re-run the commit-pinned source installer below to update. Locally assembled source builds are not redistributable release binaries.

Validation

  • Local validation: 267 existing regression tests and 8 import tests, production build/typecheck, eval typecheck, lockfile portability, and a 285-package license inventory.
  • Reviewed Electron Windows ARM64 runtime checks covered MP4/WebM import, absent/silent audio, unsupported audio/video rejection, seek/crop, cancellation, original-file preservation, exact 512 MiB input, and one-byte-over rejection.
  • A real imported recording completed local transcription and generated an activity analysis.
  • Windows x64 and ARM64 and macOS and Ubuntu passed on the exact release commit, covering commit-pinned source installation, tests, builds, compliance, and applicable packaging/architecture checks.
  • Synthetic media runtime checks were performed locally on Windows ARM64; the cross-platform workflows are not a claim that every codec or Teams export was manually exercised on every platform.

Installer script SHA-256

These hashes were calculated from the exact release commit's Git blobs.

7678f7f8d38d600f2fb732f95afeadc8aa4f9b9802989d26e2f932970795770c  install.ps1
bd8b5200a2c140c5a5ed9299d565ac72cee5420181774008977eaf75f210db1d  install.sh

Commit-pinned installation

Windows 11 x64 or ARM64

$commit="505c97ae47be5642c0e1b44ebb8d1c1530c298fa"; $env:SKILL_RECORDER_COMMIT=$commit; irm "https://raw.githubusercontent.com/microsoft/skill-recorder/$commit/install.ps1" | iex

macOS or Ubuntu

commit="505c97ae47be5642c0e1b44ebb8d1c1530c298fa"; curl -fsSL "https://raw.githubusercontent.com/microsoft/skill-recorder/$commit/install.sh" | SKILL_RECORDER_COMMIT="$commit" bash

For script inspection and hash verification before execution, use the inspect-first instructions.

farion1231/cc-switch ↗ 1 release · 1 stable
v4.0.25CC Switch v4.0.25 Stable2026-11-02T15:08:52Z

CC Switch v4.0.25

本版本为 MCP 页面增加了连通性检查，可以一键检查所有已启用的 MCP 服务器能不能连上；Codex 经本地路由用官方账号时，可以在额度快用完前自动暂停官方请求，第三方模型照常可用。另外，用量页的汇率可以自动获取，Codex 供应商表单可以设置子代理并发上限，Claude Code 和 Claude Desktop 经路由或聚合时，模型菜单会写明每个模型来自哪个供应商、实际是哪个模型，会话管理里也能删除 Kimi Code 会话了。还修复了「打开终端」混入路由和聚合设置、路由模式下 OpenCode Go 等供应商让 Claude Code 要求重新登录、给纯文本模型发过一个文件后之后每轮都报错、Windows 上供应商表单内容来回闪、启动时托盘面板打不开、Codex 用了模型映射时用量算两遍等问题。4.0 完整的更新内容请见 v4.0 发布说明。

English → | 日本語版 →

新增

MCP 连通性检查

以前 MCP 服务器配好以后，只有在编程工具里用到时才知道能不能连上（#4498）。

现在 MCP 页面顶部多了「检查连通性」按钮，会检查所有已启用的服务器；每一行的菜单里也有「测试连接」，只检查这一个。

  • 打开页面时不会自动检查，没检查过的显示「未检查」；改了某个服务器的连接配置后，它的结果会重置。
  • 检查方式是和服务器完成一次 MCP 握手，本地命令（stdio）、HTTP 和旧版 SSE 三种连接方式都支持。每个服务器最多等 30 秒，整个应用同时最多检查 3 个。
  • 本地命令启动的服务器检查完会连同它启动的子进程一起结束，不会留下后台进程。macOS 和 Linux 上不从终端启动 CC Switch 时，也能找到 npx、uvx 这类命令。

Codex 官方额度保护

Codex 用官方 ChatGPT 账号时，五小时或每周额度用完会进入 Reserve 模式。现在可以在额度快用完时先暂停官方请求，留着额度，第三方模型照常可用。

在「设置 → 应用配置 → Codex 应用增强」里打开「官方额度保护」，可以设置五小时和每周使用率的阈值（默认 95% 和 98%），任一达到就暂停新的官方请求。

  • 只对经过 CC Switch 本地路由的 Codex 官方请求生效，默认关闭。
  • 使用率直接读官方响应里自带的额度信息，正常使用时不会多发请求；刚开始用或空闲约 5 分钟后，才会先单独查询一次。
  • 查不到额度（网络问题、登录过期等）时照常放行，不会误挡。被暂停的请求会直接返回错误，不会转到故障转移队列里的其他供应商。

用量页汇率自动获取

用量页「用量设置」里的「显示」区多了「汇率来源」，可以选「手动填写」（默认）或「自动获取」。选了自动获取才会联网，汇率超过 12 小时会自动更新，获取失败时继续用上次的汇率。自动获取的汇率和手动填的分开保存，切回手动填写时你自己填的值还在。费用仍按美元记录，只在显示时换算。

Codex 子代理并发上限

Codex 供应商表单的「子代理模型」下面多了「子代理并发上限」，设置一个 Codex 会话里最多同时开多少个子代理（#9055）。这是 Codex 的全局设置，写进 config.toml 的 [agents] max_threads，切换供应商时保持不变，官方卡片上也能改；留空就用 Codex 的默认值。

模型菜单写明供应商和实际模型

Claude Code 在路由模式下，/model 菜单里每一档以前只写「Custom Sonnet model」，看不出对应哪家供应商的哪个模型。现在会写成「供应商 · 实际模型 · 窗口」（窗口只在上游标了 1M 时显示），聚合模式下的模型也一样。供应商里自己写过模型说明的，以你写的为准。

Claude Desktop 在路由和聚合模式下，模型下面的说明以前按名字里的 Opus、Sonnet 套用官方文案，聚合模型一律显示同一句。现在也改成「供应商 · 实际模型 · 窗口」。

删除 Kimi Code 会话

会话管理里可以直接删除 Kimi Code 会话了，以前只能去 Kimi Code 里删。删除方式和 Kimi Code 自己的一样，子代理的历史会一起删掉。

定价： 内置价格表新增 GPT-6.1 Terra。

变更

  • 本地路由不再接受网页发来的请求。本地路由会把请求换成你保存的供应商凭据再转发，所以网页如果能把请求发到本地路由，就能用掉你的额度。现在带浏览器来源标记的请求会被拒绝，通过普通域名指向本机的访问也不再接受。编程工具发的请求不受影响。
  • Skills「发现」页的工具栏和「已安装」页对齐（#9125）：搜索框位置和宽度一致，刷新按钮改为「刷新仓库」文字按钮，来源、仓库筛选和「只看未安装」挪进表头，「安装到」放在表头右侧。少了一行，功能不变。
  • Windows 上首选终端是 PowerShell 时，装了 PowerShell 7 就用它打开（#8992、#3271），没装才用系统自带的 Windows PowerShell。供应商卡片的「打开终端」和应用的安装、升级都是这样。
  • 预设： 千问 AI 平台和 QwenCloud 的按量付费、Token Plan 改用新的 MaaS 域名，Coding Plan 不变。

修复

  • 在路由或聚合模式下，Claude Code 卡片的「打开终端」混入了路由和聚合的设置。终端里的 Claude Code 会同时带上路由的占位凭据和这张卡片的 Key，这张卡片没配的模型档位会用上聚合里的模型名，发到这张卡片的地址上用不了；/model 菜单里也会出现其他供应商的模型。现在终端里只用这张卡片自己的设置，你的其他全局设置照常保留。
  • 路由模式下，OpenCode Go 等用 ANTHROPIC_API_KEY 存 Key 的供应商会让 Claude Code 要求确认自定义 Key，默认选「否」后就是未登录（#8111、#8104）。现在路由模式下占位凭据一律写进 ANTHROPIC_AUTH_TOKEN，发给上游的请求不变。
  • 给纯文本模型发过一个文件或音频后，之后每一轮都报 400（#6274、#6151）。现在经路由发给纯文本模型时，对话里的文件和音频会换成一段文字说明；智谱 GLM 的中文报错也能认出来。图片的处理不变。
  • Windows 上供应商表单里的名称等内容来回闪（#6420、#6349）。这是 WebView2 的自动填充在覆盖表单内容，现在已对主窗口关掉。
  • 启动后的一段时间里托盘面板打不开，也没法从托盘退出（#9154）。启动时导入会话用量会占着数据库，托盘面板要等数据。现在面板会先弹出，「打开主窗口」和「退出」马上能用，列表随后加载。
  • Codex 经路由用了模型映射时，同一个请求的用量被算两遍（#7643、#5526）。会话日志里记的是 Codex 用的模型名，路由记的是映射后的模型名，去重时对不上。现在两边能对上了。
  • Pi 官方示例的子代理用的额度没有统计进来（#8386、#8340）。现在会按每个子代理的供应商和模型分别记录，升级后会把以前的 Pi 会话重新读一遍补上。
  • 从 Pi 导入 MCP 时，Pi 不支持的服务器也被导入并启用，之后每次同步都失败（#7888）。SSE 连接方式、名字不合规、名字只差 - 和 _ 的服务器现在会跳过并提示；没装 Pi 时也不会再创建 Pi 的 MCP 配置文件。
  • 只在终端或 CC Switch 里设了代理、没设系统代理时，models.dev 价格同步一直失败（#8739、#6696）。现在改由 CC Switch 后台下载，会走你设的代理；导入模型价格和表单自动填模型参数也一样。
  • Claude Code 经路由连 xAI 时，强制联网搜索的请求被 xAI 拒绝，报 422（#8664）。
  • Pi 里的 MiniMax M3.1 Flash 思考档位（#8540）：两个 MiniMax 官方预设去掉了这个模型不接受的「关闭」和「最小」，其余档位照常发送。
